Could implement a *time-budgeted nested loop*:
- For each candidate length n (e.g., parent n, n*2 if <=16384, maybe 2048 for quick exploration), run a `optimize_sequence(a, budget)` returning best a.
- Inside optimize: maintain current a and best. Use alternating:
   1. Solve cross subproblem for current a using projected gradient/Adam for a fixed time (e.g., 5s) to get b.
   2. Evaluate mix line search (0..1) between a and b, choose best.
   3. Run self smooth gradient for a fixed time (e.g., 5s) from a.
   4. If no improvement after several cycles, restart from best with random smooth perturbation; also consider different initial shapes and lengths.
- Keep global best across all.
- At end exact validate top (maybe use our own exact for small; if evaluate_sequence available, call? Could use both). Return best.

Let's design a concrete algorithm that is likely better than previous:

**Main optimization loop**:
- Use a list of initial sequences (parent normalized, one-sided singular, maybe its smoothed variants, random perturbations).
- For each start, run a `local_optimize` with time budget.
- Keep global best.

`local_optimize(a0, budget)`:
  n fixed (parent length or selected). N fast.
  Maintain `a` probabilities, `score`.
  Maintain best.
  Use Adam state for log w: w=log(a+eps). m,v,t.
  Use three modes: SELF, CROSS, MIX.
  But to be different, implement mode based on solving subproblems with time budget and line search.

Maybe simpler: Implement two methods and choose by timing:
1. **Self Smooth Descent (SSD)**: gradient on smoothed max with increasing β.
2. **Alternating Game (AG)**: Use fixed-a best response b via multiplicative weights; blend.
Run SSD until plateau, then AG, repeat.

Detailed self:
```
def self_update(a, beta, lr):
   q=conv(a,a)
   score=...
   p=softmax(beta*(q-maxq))  # length L
   r=gradient_from_weights(a,p) # r_i
   grad=2*r
   proj=grad - dot(a, grad)
   # use multiplicative with clipping:
   step=lr*(proj - mean? already)
   exponent= -lr*proj
   exponent=clip(exponent, -clipmax, clipmax)
   an=a*exp(exponent); an/=sum
   # if not finite fallback small
   return an, score
```

Function:
```
def obj_jac_fixed_last(v, N, beta, ridge):
    w = np.empty(n); w[:-1]=v; w[-1]=0.0
    w = np.where(finite,w,0)
    a = softmax(w)
    q=fft_conv(a,N)
    p=softmax(beta*(q-maxq))
    r=grad_from_weights(a,p)
    g = 2*n*2*r? Use 2n for objective. Let g_a=4*n*r.
    grad_w = a*(g_a - dot(a,g_a))
    grad_v = grad_w[:-1]
    # add small ridge to prevent drift? Maybe `+ ridge*v`
    # objective fval=2n*max_smooth? Use `n*np.max(q)` enough? Need scale.
    return fval, grad_v
```

This alternating L-BFGS scheme:
- Start a, n.
- For cycle:
   1. Solve b = argmin smooth max conv(a,b) via L-BFGS (time/iter cap).
   2. Blend a with b to reduce self score; update a.
   3. Optionally solve b again if time.
- Also run self L-BFGS to reduce a*a directly.

**Line search blend**:
```
def blend_best(a,b,candidates=(0.1,...)):
   best=a; bestsc=score(a)
   for t in candidates:
      c=(1-t)a+t*b; if sum>0 c/=sum
      sc=score(c)
      if sc<bestsc: best=c; bestsc=sc
   return best,bestsc,t
```
Could do finer if t near boundary. Maybe use 10 grid [0,0.1.....1]. Each score one FFT. We can limit e.g., 12 evals. Good.

Let's implement upsample/downsample:
```
def resample_probability(a, m):
    n=len(a); if m==n return a
    x0=np.linspace(0,1,n); x1=np.linspace(0,1,m)
    new=np.interp(x1,x0,a)
    new=np.maximum(new,0); s=sum; if s>0 new/=s else uniform
    return new
```
For downsample, same works (average-ish). Linear C1 maybe.

def _clean_conv(c, L):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)[:L]
    if c.size != L: pad? 
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    if c.size and np.min(c) < 0: return np.maximum(c,0)
    return c
```

    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)
    return c

`soft_max_weights(vals, beta)`: length L.
```
def softmax_values(vals, beta):
    vals=np.asarray(vals)
    mx=float(np.max(vals)) if vals.size else 0
    if not finite: return None
    u=beta*(vals-mx)
    u=np.maximum(u, -745.0)
    e=np.exp(u)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if s<=0 or not finite:
       p=np.zeros_like(vals); idx=np.argmax(vals); p[idx]=1; return p
    return e/s
```
If beta small, p all positive.

`log_fixed_last_from_a(a)`:
```
def log_fixed_last(a):
    a=np.asarray(a)
    n=a.size
    floor=1e-30
    w=np.log(np.maximum(a, floor))
    w=np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    # subtract last
    w = w - w[-1]
    w = np.clip(w, -60.0, 60.0) # optional
    return w[:-1].copy()
```
If a[-1] was floor huge? fine. Clipping 60 might alter. Maybe use 70 to be safe but avoid L-BFGS issues. Use 50? The ratios may need more if sequence has zeros; but optimized sequences likely all positive. Use 700? Exp of 700 safe, but L-BFGS step can be large. Use 60 (ratio 1e26) enough.

`softmax_fixed_last(v)`:
```
def softmax_fixed_last(v):
   n=v.size+1
   w=np.empty(n)
   w[:-1]=v
   w[-1]=0.0
   w=np.where(np.isfinite(w),w,0.0)
   w-=max(w)
   e=np.exp(w)
   s=e.sum(); if s<=0: uniform
   a=e/s
   return a
```
Need ensure sum 1. If v has all -60 and last 0, a close last 1; okay.

Objective functions:
```
def self_obj_jac(v, N, n, beta):
    a = softmax_fixed_last(v)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    q = np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)
    L=2*n-1
    q = q[:L]
    if q.size==0 ...
    mx = max(0,max(q))
    u = beta*(q-mx); u=np.maximum(u,-745.0); e=np.exp(u); Z=e.sum(); if Z<=0: smooth=mx
    else smooth=mx + (1.0/beta)*np.log(Z)
    fval = (2.0*n)*smooth
    p=e/Z (if Z ok)
    r=r_from_ap(a,p,N,n)
    g = (4.0*n)*r
    grad_w = a*(g - np.dot(a,g))
    grad_v = grad_w[:-1]
    if not finite: return (1e100, zeros)
    return float(fval), grad_v
```
Wait gradient of F=2n*LSE. LSE derivative wrt a = 2r. So g=4n r. Correct. If we use fval=2n*smooth, yes. But if beta->∞, LSE→max, gradient subgradient 2n*2r_active. Good.

If beta is very small, smooth approx average. `log(Z)` with u near0, okay.

Cross objective:
```
def cross_obj_jac(v, a_fixed, N, n, beta):
   b=softmax_fixed_last(v)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a_fixed,N)
   B=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
   h=irfft(A*B,N)[:L]
   mx...
   smooth...
   p=softmax_weights(h,beta)
   r=r_from_ap(a_fixed,p,N,n)  # gradient wrt b? h_k=∑ a_{k-i}b_i; derivative wrt b_i=∑_k p_k a_{k-i}=r_i. Yes.
   g=(2.0*n)*r  # since F=2n*LSE; derivative LSE wrt b = r
   grad_w = b*(g - dot(b,g))
   return F, grad_v
```
Check factor: LSE derivative wrt b = M^T p = r. F=2n LSE, so g=2n r. Good.

But in self, LSE derivative wrt a =2r because q quadratic. Correct.

Potential sign: We minimize, so gradient as computed positive points direction to increase; optimizer subtracts.

def lfbgqsolve_self(v, N,n,beta, maxiter, time_limit, label):
    global _PHASE_DEADLINE
    _PHASE_DEADLINE=time.monotonic()+time_limit
    if not HAS_SCIPY: return v
    try:
      res=scipy_opt.minimize(self_obj_jac, v, args=(N,n,beta), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, callback=_stop_callback, options={...})
      x=res.x
      if finite return
    except: pass
    return v
```

def run_local(a0, time_limit, label):

    a=a0.copy(); v=log_fixed_last(a)
    best_a=a; best_score=fft_score...
    v_b=log_fixed_last(a)
    beta_self=20; beta_cross=20
    stall=0
    cycle=0
    while time.monotonic()-start < time_limit:
       # self L-BFGS
       if remaining > 10: v=selfolve(v,beta_self, time=min(20, remaining...))
       a=softmax(v); sc=hard; if improve best
       # cross
       if remaining > 15:
          v_b=cross_solve(v_b, a, beta_cross, time...)
          b=softmax(v_b)
          a2,sc2,tf=blend(a,b)
          if sc2<sc: a=a2; v=log_fixed_last(a); v_b=log_fixed_last(b)? maybe b initial
          update best
       # increase betas / adjust
       cycle +=1
       beta_self *=1.8; cap 20000; beta_cross*=1.8 cap
       if no improvement for 3 cycles: random smooth perturb; reset betas lower
    # final custom polish with hard active maybe 10% time
    return best_a,best_score
```

**Random smooth perturbation**:
Implement lowpass noise via FFT:
```
def random_smooth_w_noise(w, rng, sigma, low_cutoff_frac=0.1):
   n=w.size
   x=rng.normal(0,1,2*n)
   F=np.fft.rfft(x,n)
   freq=np.arange(n//2+1)
   mask=np.exp(- (freq/(max(1,n*low_cutoff_frac)))**2)
   # also zero frequency
   F[0]=0
   noise=np.fft.irfft(F*mask,n)
   # normalize to unit std
   sd=noise.std(); if sd>0 noise=noise/sd*sigma
   return w+noise
```
But w length n-1; can generate length n and slice. Use top-level.

Maybe adding smooth noise to w and then softmax. Use small sigma 0.5, 1.0.

**Custom fallback optimizer** if no scipy: We can implement a simple multiplicative update loop for self with active weights. But if no scipy, maybe still pass. Let's write a short `simple_local(a, time_limit, N)`:

However, adding too much code increases bug risk. Focus on L-BFGS.

Let's think of numerical issues in L-BFGS objective.
- If beta high, `Z=sum(e)` near1. `smooth = mx + logZ/beta`. Since e[active]=1, Z=1, smooth=mx. If multiple active, Z=count, logZ positive/beta small. Good.
- Gradient p weights get zero for underflow; if only one max coefficient, okay. But if p has zeros, gradient r maybe from one coefficient; subgradient point. L-BFGS okay.
- What if max q is at boundary coefficients with small values and active set small? okay.
- The objective has scale invariance removed by fixed last weight. But L-BFGS can still approach high w values that make a almost one-hot. If fval large maybe. Starting from parent positive.

Could L-BFGS fail due to `ValueError: positive values in x0`? no.

`scipy.optimize.minimize` with `jac=True` expects function returns (f, g). Good.

If objective returns large 1e100 and zero, L-BFGS may error "positive values". We can catch exceptions and return current v.

Pseudo:
```
def local_optimize_lfbgls(a0, time_limit, label):
    global _STOP_DEADLINE
    start_all = time.monotonic()
    n = len(a0)
    if n<16: n=4096 a0=uniform
    N=next_fast_len(2*n-1); L=2*n-1
    a=normalize(a0)

```
def blend_search(a,b,base_score,N,n):
   best=a; bestsc=base_score
   ts=(0.1,0.25,0.4,0.5,0.65,0.8,0.9,1.0)
   for t in ts:
       c=(1-t)a+t*b
       s=sum; if s<=0 continue
       c=c/s
       sc=fft_score(c,N,n)
       if sc<bestsc: best=c; bestsc=sc
   return best,bestsc
```

Need be careful in `lfbgls_minimize_self`: if `time_limit <=0` return v. `maxiter` maybe based on n: `maxiter = 80 if n>8000 else 160`. Use callback time.

```
def lfbgls_minimize_self(v,N,n,beta,time_limit,maxiter=None):
   if not HAS_SCIPY or time_limit<=0: return v
   if maxiter is None: maxiter = 60 if n>10000 else 120
   _STOP_DEADLINE = min(time.monotonic()+time_limit, _GLOBAL_DEADLINE-2.0)
   try:
      res = _scipy_opt.minimize(self_obj_jac, v.copy(), args=(N,n,beta), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, callback=stop_callback, options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'maxls':20, 'ftol':1e-8, 'gtol':1e-5, 'maxcor':20})
      if res.x is not None:
          x=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64)
          if np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return np.clip(x,-70,70)
   except Exception: pass
   return v
```

```
def local_optimize(a0, time_limit, label, rng):
   start=time.monotonic()
   n=len(a0)
   if n<32: a0=np.ones(4096)/4096; n=4096
   if n>MAX_N: a0=resample_probability(a0, MAX_N); n=MAX_N
   N=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
   a=normalize_nonneg(a0)
   best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=fft_score(a,N,n)
   v=log_fixed_last(a)
   v_b=v.copy()
   betas_self=[30,60,120,240,480,960,1920,3840,7680,15360,30000]
   betas_cross=[30,60,120,240,480,960,1920,3840,7680,15360]
   bi=0; noimp=0; cycle=0
   print(label start score)
   while True:
      elapsed=time.monotonic()-start
      if elapsed >= time_limit or time.monotonic() >= _GLOBAL_DEADLINE-5: break
      rem = min(time_limit-elapsed, _GLOBAL_DEADLINE-5 - time.monotonic())
      if rem < 5: break
      # self
      beta = betas_self[min(bi,len(betas_self)-1)]
      ph = min(40.0, max(3.0, rem*0.45))
      v2 = lfbgls_minimize_self(v,N,n,beta,ph)
      a2 = softmax_fixed_last(v2)
      sc2 = fft_score(a2,N,n)
      improved = np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc - 1e-12
      if improved:
         best_a=a2.copy(); best_sc=sc2; noimp=0
      else:
         noimp += 1
      # choose current for next: if sc2 reasonable or best improved
      if np.isfinite(sc2) and (improved or sc2 < best_sc + 0.02):
          current=a2; cur_sc=sc2
      else:
          current=best_a.copy(); cur_sc=best_sc
      v = log_fixed_last(current)
      # cross if rem>10
      if np.isfinite(cur_sc) and rem > 12:
         beta_c = betas_cross[min(bi, len(betas_cross)-1)]
         ph_c = min(40.0, max(3.0, rem*0.35))
         v_b2 = lfbgls_minimize_cross(v_b, current, N,n,beta_c,ph_c)
         b = softmax_fixed_last(v_b2)
         cand, cand_sc = blend_search(current, b, cur_sc, N,n)
         if np.isfinite(cand_sc) and cand_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
             best_a=cand.copy(); best_sc=cand_sc; noimp=0
             v_b = v_b2.copy()
             if cand_sc < cur_sc:
                 current=cand.copy(); cur_sc=cand_sc; v=log_fixed_last(current)
         else:
             noimp += 1
         # if candidate not best but improves current, maybe accept
         if np.isfinite(cand_sc) and cand_sc < cur_sc:
             current=cand.copy(); cur_sc=cand_sc; v=log_fixed_last(current)
         # keep b state if b good? v_b = v_b2
         if np.all(np.isfinite(v_b2)):
             v_b=v_b2.copy()
      # beta progression/restarts
      cycle +=1
      if cycle % 4 == 0 or noimp >= 3:
          if bi < len(betas_self)-1:
             bi +=1
      print(f"{label} c{cycle} best {best_sc:.7f} cur {cur_sc:.7f} beta {beta} {time.monotonic()-start:.1f}")
      if noimp > 8:
         # perturb
         w = log_fixed_last(best_a)
         w = random_smooth_perturb(w, rng, 0.5)
         a_p = softmax_fixed_last(w)
         sc_p=fft_score(a_p,N,n)
         if np.isfinite(sc_p):
            current=a_p.copy(); cur_sc=sc_p
            v = log_fixed_last(current)
            v_b = v.copy()
            bi = min(bi, 4)  # lower beta
            noimp = 0
         else:
            current=best_a.copy(); v=log_fixed_last(current)
   # final high beta if time
   rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic()-start)
   if rem > 10 and HAS_SCIPY:
      v=log_fixed_last(best_a)
      ph=min(30, rem*0.7)
      v2=lfbgls_minimize_self(v,N,n,30000,ph)
      a2=softmax_fixed_last(v2); sc2=fft_score(...); if better update
   return best_a,best_sc
```

**Random smooth perturb function**: Need `rng`. Top-level. Use `np.fft.irfft` with random complex? Could just add `sigma * smooth_noise` where smooth noise is Gaussian convolved with Gaussian kernel via FFT or use random cosine modes. Simple:
```
def random_smooth_perturb(w, rng, sigma):
    n=w.size
    # generate white noise and low-pass with cutoff fraction 0.05
    x=rng.normal(0,1.0,size=2*n)
    F=np.fft.rfft(x)
    freq=np.arange(F.size)
    cutoff=max(2.0, n*0.1)
    mask=np.exp(-0.5*(freq/cutoff)**2)
    mask[0]=0.0
    noise=np.fft.irfft(F*mask, 2*n)[:n]
    sd=float(np.std(noise))
    if sd>0: noise *= (sigma/sd)
    return w + noise
```
Use maybe too slow once. okay.

```
def simple_local(a0, time_limit, label, rng):
   ...
   m = zeros; v=zeros; t=0; lr=0.05; beta=20
   while time:
      a=...
      q=conv; mx; p=softmax_values(q,beta)
      r=r_from_A_p(A,p,N,n)
      grad=4*n*r
      proj=grad - dot(a,grad)
      # Adam in log using w=log_fixed? Instead use multiplicative:
      for mult in (1,0.5,2,0.25):
         step = lr*mult
         expon = -step * proj / (np.max(np.abs(proj))+1e-300)
         a_new = a * np.exp(np.clip(expon,-10,10)); normalize
         sc=score; if sc<best: accept
      if no improve: beta=min(20000,beta*1.2); lr=max(0.01,lr*0.9)
   return best
```
But using `proj` scale to max ensures stable.

`self_obj_jac` gradient:
- a=softmax(w), sum1.
- q=conv(a,a).
- p=softmax(β(q-mx)) sum1.
- r_i=∑_j a_j p_{i+j}.
- `grad_a = 4*n*r`
- For softmax parameter w, gradient wrt w_j = a_j*(grad_a_j - ∑ a_i grad_a_i). Since w fixed last, return first n-1. This is exact if `grad_a` derivative of F wrt a without sum constraint. Since objective scale invariant? We normalize a via softmax, sum constraint enforced. The chain rule for w is a_j (dF/da_j - ∑ a_i dF/da_i). Correct because da_j/dw_k = δ_jk a_j - a_j a_k.
- Good.

`cross_obj_jac`:
- h=conv(a_fixed,b), linear in b.
- p=softmax β(h-mx).
- r_i=∑_j a_{?} p? We use `r_from_A_p(A_fixed,p)`: r_i=∑_j a_j p_{i+j}. But derivative of h_k wrt b_i = a_{k-i}. Summing p_k: r_i=∑_k p_k a_{k-i}. Let j=k-i => a_j p_{i+j}. Yes. Good.
- grad_b = 2n*r? Objective F=2n*LSE_h; LSE derivative wrt b=r. Good.
- gradient wrt w = b*(grad_b - dot(b,grad_b)). Good.

Detailed function implementations:

```
def normalize_nonneg(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size==0: arr=np.ones(1)
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
    s=float(np.sum(arr))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=1e-300:
        arr=np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64); s=float(arr.size)
    return arr/s
```

`softmax_values`:
```
def softmax_values(vals, beta):
    vals=np.asarray(vals,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if vals.size==0: return np.zeros(0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(vals)):
        vals=np.where(np.isfinite(vals), vals, 0.0)
    mx=float(np.max(vals))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        # fallback uniform over finite max
        mask=np.isfinite(vals); mx=max(vals[mask]) if mask.any() else 0
    u=beta*(vals-mx)
    u=np.where(np.isfinite(u), u, -745.0)
    u=np.maximum(u, -745.0)
    e=np.exp(u)
    s=float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0.0:
       p=np.zeros(vals.shape,dtype=float)
       idx=int(np.argmax(vals)); p[idx]=1.0; return p
    p=e/s
    return p
```
If beta=0? u zeros, p uniform. Good.

`fft_conv(a,N)`:
```
def fft_conv(a,N):
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)
    return c
```
No cleaning.

`fft_score`:
```
def fft_score(a,N,n):
    c=fft_conv(a,N)
    L=2*n-1
    if L<=0: return 0
    if c.size < L: c=np.pad? but c size N >= L.
    seg=c[:L]
    mx=float(np.max(seg)) if seg.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(mx): return float('inf')
    if mx<0: mx=0.0
    return float(2.0*n*mx)
```
If a sum not 1, not score. We assume.

`r_from_A_p(A,p,N,n)`:
```
def r_from_A_p(A,p,N,n):
    L=2*n-1
    pr=p[:L][::-1].copy()
    P=np.fft.rfft(pr,N)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(A*P,N)
    idx=L-1 - np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    r=conv[idx]
    r=np.where(np.isfinite(r), r, 0.0)
    return r
```
This allocates idx array each call. Could precompute and pass idx? To optimize, define `make_r_indices(n)` and pass. In objective functions, we can compute idx once? Top-level no state? Could pass idx as arg to obj. For performance, modify objective to accept `idx`? `minimize` args can include idx. Let's do:
- `make_r_indices(n): return L-1 - np.arange(n,dtype=np.int64)`
- `self_obj_jac(v,N,n,beta,idx)`, `r_from_A_p_indexed(A,p,N,n,idx)`.
Similarly cross.
This avoids creating each call.

`self_obj_jac(v,N,n,beta,idx)`:
```
def self_obj_jac(v,N,n,beta,idx):
   a=softmax_fixed_last(v)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   q=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)
   L=2*n-1
   seg=q[:L]
   mx=float(np.max(seg)) if seg.size else 0.0
   if not finite mx: return (1e100, zeros)
   if mx<0: mx=0.0
   u=beta*(seg-mx); u=np.maximum(np.where(finite,u,-745),-745)
   e=np.exp(u); Z=float(e.sum())
   if Z<=0 or not finite:
       smooth=mx
       p=np.zeros(L); idxmax=int(np.argmax(seg)); p[idxmax]=1.0
   else:
       smooth=mx + (1.0/beta)*np.log(Z)
       p=e/Z
   fval=2.0*n*smooth
   r=r_from_A_p_indexed(A,p,N,n,idx)
   grad=4.0*n*r
   mean=float(np.dot(a,grad))
   gw=a*(grad-mean)
   gw=np.where(np.isfinite(gw), gw, 0.0)
   fval=float(fval) if finite else 1e100
   if not finite fval: return (1e100, np.zeros(n-1))
   return (fval, gw[:-1])
```
If beta very small (0) division by zero. We won't use beta 0. Betas start 30.

   mx...
   p=...
   smooth...
   r=r_from_A_p_indexed(A_fixed,p,N,n,idx)
   grad=2.0*n*r
   mean=dot(b,grad)
   gw=b*(grad-mean)
   return 2*n*smooth, gw[:-1]
```

```
def simple_local(a0,time_limit,label,rng):
   a=normalize; best; N; idx
   lr=0.05; beta=30; noimp=0; cycle=0
   while time:
      A=rfft(a,N); q=irfft(A*A,N)[:L]; mx...
      p=softmax_values(q,beta)
      r=r_from_A_p_indexed(A,p,N,n,idx)
      grad=4*n*r; proj=grad-dot(a,grad)
      scale=max(np.max(np.abs(proj)),1e-300)
      improved=False
      for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25,2.0):
         step=lr*mult
         ex=np.clip(-step*proj/scale, -10,10)
         an=np.maximum(a*np.exp(ex),0); s=an.sum(); if s>0: an/=s
         sc=fft_score(an,N,n)
         if np.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_sc-1e-12:
             a=an; best_sc=sc; best_a=an.copy(); improved=True; break
      if improved: noimp=0; lr=min(0.3,lr*1.05)
      else: noimp+=1; lr=max(0.01,lr*0.9)
         if noimp%5==0: beta=min(30000,beta*1.5)
      if noimp>20: perturb...
   return best_a,best_sc
```
This is O(1 FFT per trial) but 4 trials per cycle. okay if fallback.

**`resample_probability`**:
```
def resample_probability(a,m):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    n=a.size
    if m<=1: return np.ones(1)
    if m==n: return a
    x0=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    x1=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    b=np.interp(x1,x0,a)
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0); s=sum; if s<=0: uniform
    return b/s
```
If n=1, x0 [0], interp. ok.

def add_top_candidate(top, a, score):
    if not np.isfinite(score): return top
    a = np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    max_items=16
    if len(top)<max_items:
        top.append((score,a)); return top
    worst=0; worst_score=top[0][0]
    for i in range(1,len(top)):
        if top[i][0] > worst_score:
            worst_score=top[i][0]; worst=i
    if score < worst_score:
        top[worst]=(score,a)
    return top
```
No closure.

`exact_score`:
```
def exact_score(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   n=a.size
   if n==0: return inf
   a=np.where(finite,a,0); a=np.maximum(a,0)
   s=float(np.sum(a))
   if not finite or s<0.01: return inf
   b=np.convolve(a,a)
   if b.size==0: return inf
   mx=float(np.max(b))
   val=2.0*n*mx/(s*s)
   return val if finite else inf
```
Use sum1.

`lfbgls_minimize_self` if `time_limit` maybe negative. Use `phase_end`.

```
def lfbgls_minimize_self(v, N, n, beta, idx, time_limit):
    global _STOP_DEADLINE
    if not HAS_SCIPY: return v
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if size mismatch? if v.size != n-1 return v
    now=time.monotonic()
    if time_limit <= 0.5: return v
    phase_end = min(now + float(time_limit), _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 2.0)
    if phase_end <= now: return v
    m_iter = 70 if n > 10000 else (140 if n > 5000 else 220)
    _STOP_DEADLINE = phase_end
    try:
       res = _scipy_opt.minimize(self_obj_jac, v.copy(), args=(N,n,beta,idx), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, callback=stop_callback, options={'maxiter': int(m_iter), 'maxls': 20, 'ftol': 1e-8, 'gtol': 1e-5, 'maxcor': 20})
       x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       if x.size == n-1 and np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
          return np.clip(x, -70.0, 70.0)
    except Exception:
       pass
    return v
```

def local_optimize(a0, time_limit, label, rng):
    start=time.monotonic()
    a=np.asarray(a0,...).ravel()
    if a.size < 64: a=np.ones(4096)/4096
    if a.size > MAX_N: a=resample_probability(a,MAX_N)
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    n=a.size
    N=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    idx=make_r_indices(n)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=fft_score(a,N,n)
    if not finite: a uniform...
    v=log_fixed_last(a); v_b=v.copy()
    bi=0; noimp=0; cycle=0
    print...
    while ...:
       rem_global = _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 5.0 - time.monotonic()
       if rem_global <=0: break
       rem = min(time_limit - (time.monotonic()-start), rem_global)
       if rem < 6: break
       beta=SELF_BETAS[min(bi,len-1)]
       phase=min(45,max(5,rem*0.42))
       v2=lfbgls_minimize_self(v,N,n,beta,idx,phase)
       a2=softmax_fixed_last(v2)
       sc2=fft_score(a2,N,n)
       improved_cycle=False
       if finite and sc2 < best_sc - 1e-12:
          best_a=a2; best_sc=sc2; improved_cycle=True
       if finite and (improved_cycle or sc2 < 1.01*best_sc):
          current=a2; cur_sc=sc2
       else:
          current=best_a.copy(); cur_sc=best_sc
       v=log_fixed_last(current)
       if rem > 12:
          beta_c = CROSS_BETAS[min(bi,len-1)]
          phase_c=min(35,max(5,rem*0.32))
          A_cur=np.fft.rfft(current,N)
          v_b2=lfbgls_minimize_cross(v_b,A_cur,N,n,beta_c,idx,phase_c)
          b=softmax_fixed_last(v_b2)
          cand,cand_sc=blend_search(current,b,cur_sc,N,n)
          if finite and cand_sc < best_sc -1e-12:
             best_a=cand; best_sc=cand_sc; improved_cycle=True
             if cand_sc < cur_sc:
                current=cand.copy(); cur_sc=cand_sc; v=log_fixed_last(current)
          elif finite and cand_sc < cur_sc:
             current=cand.copy(); cur_sc=cand_sc; v=log_fixed_last(current)
          if np.all(np.isfinite(v_b2)): v_b=v_b2.copy()
       if improved_cycle: noimp=0
       else: noimp +=1
       if noimp >= 3 and bi < len(SELF_BETAS)-1: bi +=1
       if noimp > 9:
          w=log_fixed_last(best_a)
          w=random_smooth_perturb(w,rng,0.5)
          ap=softmax_fixed_last(w)
          scp=fft_score(ap,N,n)
          if finite:
             current=ap.copy(); cur_sc=scp; v=log_fixed_last(current); v_b=v.copy(); bi=min(bi,4); noimp=1
          else: current=best_a.copy(); cur_sc=best_sc; v=log_fixed_last(current)
       cycle +=1
       if cycle % 2 ==0 or improved_cycle:
           print(f"[{label}] score={best_sc:.7f} cur={cur_sc:.7f} beta={beta} {time.monotonic()-start:.1f}s")
    # final high beta

    return best_a,best_sc

def blend_search(a,b,base_score,N,n):
   a=np.asarray(a); b=np.asarray(b)
   best=a.astype(float).copy(); bestsc=base_score
   if not finite: bestsc=fft_score(a)

      s=sum; if s>0: c/=s

   return best,bestsc

`random_smooth_perturb`: if w size 0 return. Need use `np.fft.rfft` length maybe 2*n for symmetry. Code:
```
def random_smooth_perturb(w, rng, sigma):
    w=np.asarray(w,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=w.size
    if n==0: return w
    m=2*n
    x=rng.normal(0.0,1.0,size=m)
    F=np.fft.rfft(x)
    freq=np.arange(F.size, dtype=np.float64)
    cutoff=max(3.0, 0.08*n)
    mask=np.exp(-0.5*(freq/cutoff)**2)
    mask[0]=0.0
    noise=np.fft.irfft(F*mask, m)[:n]
    sd=float(np.std(noise))
    if sd > 1e-300:
       noise = noise*(sigma/sd)
    else:
       noise=rng.normal(0.0,sigma,size=n)
    out=w+noise
    out=np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    return np.clip(out, -70.0,70.0)
```
If sigma negative? use abs.

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative coefficient sequences minimizing

    2 * n * max(np.convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a) ** 2)

by keeping sum(a) = 1 and using FFT acceleration for the linear
self-convolution.  The optimizer uses smooth-max continuations with
L-BFGS on a log-simplex parameterization, alternating with a fixed-a
cross-convolution best-response subproblem and line-search blends.
Multiple lengths and smooth random perturbations are used to reduce
the chance of getting stuck in a bad local minimum.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    import scipy.optimize as _scipy_opt
    HAS_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    HAS_SCIPY = False

MAX_N = 12000
MAX_EXACT_N = 8192

_STOP_DEADLINE = 0.0
_GLOBAL_DEADLINE = 0.0

SELF_BETAS = (30.0, 60.0, 120.0, 240.0, 480.0, 960.0, 1920.0, 3840.0, 7680.0, 15360.0, 30000.0)
CROSS_BETAS = (30.0, 60.0, 120.0, 240.0, 480.0, 960.0, 1920.0, 3840.0, 7680.0, 15360.0, 30000.0)


def next_fast_len(m):
    """Smallest power of two >= m."""
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def normalize_nonneg(a):
    """Return finite nonnegative 1D array normalized to sum 1."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        arr = np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(arr.size)
    return arr / s


def make_uniform(n):
    n = max(1, int(n))
    return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)


def softmax_values(vals, beta):
    """Probability vector proportional to exp(beta * vals), stable."""
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if vals.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(vals)):
        vals = np.where(np.isfinite(vals), vals, 0.0)

    mx = float(np.max(vals))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        mx = 0.0

    if beta <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones(vals.shape, dtype=np.float64)
        p /= float(p.size)
        return p

    u = beta * (vals - mx)
    u = np.where(np.isfinite(u), u, -745.0)
    u = np.maximum(u, -745.0)
    e = np.exp(u)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros(vals.shape, dtype=np.float64)
        idx = int(np.argmax(vals))
        p[idx] = 1.0
        return p
    return e / s


def fft_conv(a, N):
    """Full linear convolution using zero-padded FFT."""
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)


def fft_score(a, N, n):
    """Score for a probability vector: 2*n*max(conv(a,a))."""
    c = fft_conv(a, N)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if L <= 0:
        return 0.0
    seg = c[:L]
    if seg.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    mx = float(np.max(seg))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return float("inf")
    if mx < 0.0:
        mx = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * float(n) * mx)


def make_r_indices(n):
    """Indices for r_i = sum_j a_j p_{i+j} from a reversed convolution."""
    L = 2 * n - 1
    return L - 1 - np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)


def r_from_A_p(A, p, n, idx):
    """
    Given A = FFT(a), and p over lags 0..2n-2, compute
    r_i = sum_j a_j p_{i+j}, i=0..n-1.
    """
    L = 2 * n - 1
    pr = p[:L][::-1].copy()
    P = np.fft.rfft(pr, len(A) * 2 - 1 if False else 0)  # placeholder, replaced below
    # Need FFT length from A: A length is N//2+1, recover N.
    N = 2 * (A.size - 1)
    P = np.fft.rfft(pr, N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A * P, N)
    r = conv[idx]
    r = np.where(np.isfinite(r), r, 0.0)
    return r


def log_fixed_last(a):
    """Log-weights whose last entry is fixed to 0."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    floor = 1e-30
    w = np.log(np.maximum(a, floor))
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    w = w - w[-1]
    w = np.clip(w, -70.0, 70.0)
    return w[:-1].astype(np.float64, copy=True)


def softmax_fixed_last(v):
    """Probability vector from log-weight vector with last weight = 0."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size + 1
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    w = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    w[:-1] = v
    w[-1] = 0.0
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)

    mx = float(np.max(w))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        mx = 0.0
    w = w - mx
    e = np.exp(w)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        out = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        return out / float(n)
    out = e / s
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s2 = float(np.sum(out))
    if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 <= 0.0:
        out = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        return out / float(n)
    return out / s2


def self_obj_jac(v, N, n, beta, idx):
    """
    Smoothed-max self-convolution objective and gradient w.r.t. first n-1
    log-weights (last fixed to zero).
    """
    a = softmax_fixed_last(v)
    if a.size != n:
        z = np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
        return 1e100, z

    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    q = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    seg = q[:L]
    if seg.size == 0:
        z = np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
        return 1e100, z

    mx = float(np.max(seg))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        mx = 0.0
    if mx < 0.0:
        mx = 0.0

    if beta <= 0.0:
        p = softmax_values(seg, 1.0)
        smooth = mx
    else:
        u = beta * (seg - mx)
        u = np.where(np.isfinite(u), u, -745.0)
        u = np.maximum(u, -745.0)
        e = np.exp(u)
        Z = float(np.sum(e))
        if not np.isfinite(Z) or Z <= 0.0:
            p = np.zeros(seg.shape, dtype=np.float64)
            idxmax = int(np.argmax(seg))
            p[idxmax] = 1.0
            smooth = mx
        else:
            p = e / Z
            smooth = mx + (1.0 / beta) * float(np.log(Z))

    fval = 2.0 * float(n) * smooth
    if not np.isfinite(fval):
        fval = 1e100

    r = r_from_A_p(A, p, n, idx)
    grad_a = 4.0 * float(n) * r
    mean_grad = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    grad_w = a * (grad_a - mean_grad)
    grad_w = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_w), grad_w, 0.0)

    if grad_w.size < n - 1:
        grad_v = np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        grad_v = grad_w[: n - 1]

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_v)):
        grad_v = np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)

    return float(fval), grad_v


def cross_obj_jac(v, A_fixed, N, n, beta, idx):
    """
    For fixed a (with FFT A_fixed), smooth-max objective and gradient for
    b in simplex minimizing max(a*b).
    """
    b = softmax_fixed_last(v)
    if b.size != n:
        z = np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
        return 1e100, z

    B = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
    h = np.fft.irfft(A_fixed * B, N)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    seg = h[:L]
    if seg.size == 0:
        z = np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
        return 1e100, z

    mx = float(np.max(seg))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        mx = 0.0
    if mx < 0.0:
        mx = 0.0

    if beta <= 0.0:
        p = softmax_values(seg, 1.0)
        smooth = mx
    else:
        u = beta * (seg - mx)
        u = np.where(np.isfinite(u), u, -745.0)
        u = np.maximum(u, -745.0)
        e = np.exp(u)
        Z = float(np.sum(e))
        if not np.isfinite(Z) or Z <= 0.0:
            p = np.zeros(seg.shape, dtype=np.float64)
            idxmax = int(np.argmax(seg))
            p[idxmax] = 1.0
            smooth = mx
        else:
            p = e / Z
            smooth = mx + (1.0 / beta) * float(np.log(Z))

    fval = 2.0 * float(n) * smooth
    if not np.isfinite(fval):
        fval = 1e100

    r = r_from_A_p(A_fixed, p, n, idx)
    grad_b = 2.0 * float(n) * r
    mean_grad = float(np.dot(b, grad_b))
    grad_w = b * (grad_b - mean_grad)
    grad_w = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_w), grad_w, 0.0)

    if grad_w.size < n - 1:
        grad_v = np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        grad_v = grad_w[: n - 1]

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_v)):
        grad_v = np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)

    return float(fval), grad_v


def stop_callback(x):
    """Stop L-BFGS-B when the phase/global deadline is exceeded."""
    if time.monotonic() > _STOP_DEADLINE:
        return True
    return False


def lfbgls_minimize_self(v, N, n, beta, idx, time_limit):
    """Run L-BFGS-B for self smoothed-max optimization."""
    global _STOP_DEADLINE

    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if v.size != n - 1:
        if n <= 1:
            return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
        return v

    if not HAS_SCIPY:
        return v

    now = time.monotonic()
    if time_limit <= 0.5:
        return v

    phase_end = min(now + float(time_limit), _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 2.0)
    if phase_end <= now:
        return v

    if n > 10000:
        maxiter = 70
    elif n > 5000:
        maxiter = 140
    else:
        maxiter = 220

    _STOP_DEADLINE = phase_end
    try:
        res = _scipy_opt.minimize(
            self_obj_jac,
            v.copy(),
            args=(N, n, float(beta), idx),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            callback=stop_callback,
            options={
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "maxls": 20,
                "ftol": 1e-8,
                "gtol": 1e-5,
                "maxcor": 20,
            },
        )
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if x.size == n - 1 and np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            return np.clip(x, -70.0, 70.0)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return v


def lfbgls_minimize_cross(v, A_fixed, N, n, beta, idx, time_limit):
    """Run L-BFGS-B for fixed-a cross smoothed-max optimization."""
    global _STOP_DEADLINE

    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if v.size != n - 1:
        if n <= 1:
            return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
        return v

    if not HAS_SCIPY:
        return v

    now = time.monotonic()
    if time_limit <= 0.5:
        return v

    phase_end = min(now + float(time_limit), _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 2.0)
    if phase_end <= now:
        return v

    if n > 10000:
        maxiter = 80
    elif n > 5000:
        maxiter = 160
    else:
        maxiter = 260

    _STOP_DEADLINE = phase_end
    try:
        res = _scipy_opt.minimize(
            cross_obj_jac,
            v.copy(),
            args=(A_fixed, N, n, float(beta), idx),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            callback=stop_callback,
            options={
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "maxls": 20,
                "ftol": 1e-8,
                "gtol": 1e-5,
                "maxcor": 20,
            },
        )
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if x.size == n - 1 and np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            return np.clip(x, -70.0, 70.0)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return v


def blend_search(a, b, base_score, N, n):
    """Choose the best convex combination of a and b by FFT score."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size != b.size or a.size == 0:
        return a.copy(), base_score

    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = base_score
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_sc = fft_score(a, N, n)

    ts = (0.10, 0.25, 0.40, 0.55, 0.70, 0.85, 1.00)
    for t in ts:
        c = (1.0 - float(t)) * a + float(t) * b
        s = float(np.sum(c))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            continue
        c = c / s
        sc = fft_score(c, N, n)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and (not np.isfinite(best_sc) or sc < best_sc):
            best = c.copy()
            best_sc = sc
    return best, best_sc


def random_smooth_perturb(w, rng, sigma):
    """Add a low-pass random perturbation to log-weights."""
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = w.size
    if n == 0:
        return w

    m = max(4, 2 * n)
    x = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=m)
    F = np.fft.rfft(x)
    freq = np.arange(F.size, dtype=np.float64)
    cutoff = max(3.0, 0.08 * float(n))
    mask = np.exp(-0.5 * (freq / cutoff) ** 2)
    mask[0] = 0.0
    noise = np.fft.irfft(F * mask, m)[:n]

    sd = float(np.std(noise))
    if not np.isfinite(sd) or sd <= 1e-300:
        noise = rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), size=n)
    else:
        noise = noise * (float(sigma) / sd)

    out = w + noise
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    return np.clip(out, -70.0, 70.0)


def resample_probability(a, m):
    """Resample a probability vector to length m by linear interpolation."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()

    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.interp(x1, x0, a)
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(b))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return make_uniform(m)
    return b / s


def make_one_sided(n):
    """Approximate one-sided 1/sqrt(i) shape."""
    n = max(2, int(n))
    x = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5
    w = -0.5 * np.log(x)
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    w -= float(np.max(w))
    e = np.exp(w)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return make_uniform(n)
    return e / s


def make_two_sided_mild(n):
    """Mild two-sided shape, used only as a diverse start."""
    n = max(2, int(n))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5
    j = (n + 0.5) - i
    j = np.maximum(j, 1e-12)
    w = -0.25 * np.log(i) - 0.25 * np.log(j)
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    w -= float(np.max(w))
    e = np.exp(w)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return make_uniform(n)
    return e / s


def simple_local(a0, time_limit, label, rng):
    """Fallback multiplicative optimizer if SciPy/L-BFGS-B is unavailable."""
    start = time.monotonic()
    a = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    if a.size < 64:
        a = make_uniform(4096)
    if a.size > MAX_N:
        a = resample_probability(a, MAX_N)

    n = a.size
    N = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
    idx = make_r_indices(n)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = fft_score(a, N, n)
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        a = make_uniform(n)
        best_a = a.copy()
        best_sc = fft_score(a, N, n)

    lr = 0.05
    beta = 30.0
    noimp = 0
    cycle = 0

    print(f"[{label}] fallback start {best_sc:.7f} n={n}", flush=True)

    while True:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
        if elapsed >= time_limit or time.monotonic() >= _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 5.0:
            break
        rem_global = max(0.0, _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 5.0 - time.monotonic())
        rem = min(time_limit - elapsed, rem_global)
        if rem < 3.0:
            break

        A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        q = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[: 2 * n - 1]
        p = softmax_values(q, beta)
        r = r_from_A_p(A, p, n, idx)
        grad_a = 4.0 * float(n) * r
        proj = grad_a - float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(proj))) if proj.size else 1.0
        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-300:
            scale = 1e-300

        improved = False
        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 2.0):
            step = lr * mult
            ex = -step * proj / scale
            ex = np.where(np.isfinite(ex), ex, 0.0)
            ex = np.clip(ex, -10.0, 10.0)
            an = a * np.exp(ex)
            an = np.where(np.isfinite(an), an, 0.0)
            an = np.maximum(an, 0.0)
            s = float(np.sum(an))
            if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
                continue
            an = an / s
            sc = fft_score(an, N, n)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                a = an
                best_a = an.copy()
                best_sc = sc
                improved = True
                break

        if improved:
            noimp = 0
            lr = min(0.30, lr * 1.05)
        else:
            noimp += 1
            lr = max(0.01, lr * 0.90)
            if noimp % 7 == 0:
                beta = min(30000.0, beta * 1.3)

        if noimp > 25:
            w = log_fixed_last(best_a)
            w = random_smooth_perturb(w, rng, 0.5)
            ap = softmax_fixed_last(w)
            scp = fft_score(ap, N, n)
            if np.isfinite(scp):
                a = ap
                if scp < best_sc:
                    best_a = ap.copy()
                    best_sc = scp
            else:
                a = best_a.copy()
            noimp = 2

        cycle += 1
        if cycle % 20 == 0 or improved:
            print(f"[{label}] fallback score={best_sc:.7f} {elapsed:.1f}s", flush=True)

    return best_a, best_sc


def local_optimize(a0, time_limit, label, rng):
    """
    Local optimization from a start sequence.
    Uses L-BFGS smoothed-max self optimization and alternating fixed-a
    cross best response, with line-search blends and restarts.
    """
    if not HAS_SCIPY:
        return simple_local(a0, time_limit, label, rng)

    start = time.monotonic()

    a = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    if a.size < 64:
        a = make_uniform(4096)
    if a.size > MAX_N:
        a = resample_probability(a, MAX_N)

    n = a.size
    N = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
    idx = make_r_indices(n)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = fft_score(best_a, N, n)
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_a = make_uniform(n).copy()
        best_sc = fft_score(best_a, N, n)

    current = best_a.copy()
    cur_sc = best_sc
    v = log_fixed_last(current)
    v_b = v.copy()

    beta_idx = 0
    noimp = 0
    cycle = 0

    print(f"[{label}] start score={best_sc:.7f} n={n} limit={float(time_limit):.1f}s", flush=True)

    while True:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
        if elapsed >= time_limit or time.monotonic() >= _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 5.0:
            break
        rem_global = max(0.0, _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 5.0 - time.monotonic())
        rem = min(float(time_limit) - elapsed, rem_global)
        if rem < 6.0:
            break

        beta = SELF_BETAS[min(beta_idx, len(SELF_BETAS) - 1)]
        phase_self = min(45.0, max(5.0, rem * 0.42))
        v2 = lfbgls_minimize_self(v, N, n, beta, idx, phase_self)
        a2 = softmax_fixed_last(v2)
        sc2 = fft_score(a2, N, n)

        improved_cycle = False
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_a = a2.copy()
            best_sc = sc2
            improved_cycle = True

        if np.isfinite(sc2) and (improved_cycle or sc2 < 1.01 * best_sc):
            current = a2.copy()
            cur_sc = sc2
        else:
            current = best_a.copy()
            cur_sc = best_sc

        v = log_fixed_last(current)

        if rem > 12.0:
            beta_c = CROSS_BETAS[min(beta_idx, len(CROSS_BETAS) - 1)]
            phase_cross = min(35.0, max(5.0, rem * 0.32))
            A_cur = np.fft.rfft(current, N)
            v_b2 = lfbgls_minimize_cross(v_b, A_cur, N, n, beta_c, idx, phase_cross)
            b = softmax_fixed_last(v_b2)
            cand, cand_sc = blend_search(current, b, cur_sc, N, n)

            if np.isfinite(cand_sc):
                if cand_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_a = cand.copy()
                    best_sc = cand_sc
                    improved_cycle = True
                    if cand_sc < cur_sc:
                        current = cand.copy()
                        cur_sc = cand_sc
                        v = log_fixed_last(current)
                elif cand_sc < cur_sc:
                    current = cand.copy()
                    cur_sc = cand_sc
                    v = log_fixed_last(current)

            if v_b2.size == n - 1 and np.all(np.isfinite(v_b2)):
                v_b = v_b2.copy()

        if improved_cycle:
            noimp = 0
        else:
            noimp += 1

        if noimp >= 3 and beta_idx < len(SELF_BETAS) - 1:
            beta_idx += 1

        if noimp > 9:
            w = log_fixed_last(best_a)
            w = random_smooth_perturb(w, rng, 0.5)
            ap = softmax_fixed_last(w)
            scp = fft_score(ap, N, n)
            if np.isfinite(scp):
                current = ap.copy()
                cur_sc = scp
                v = log_fixed_last(current)
                v_b = v.copy()
                beta_idx = min(beta_idx, 4)
                noimp = 1
            else:
                current = best_a.copy()
                cur_sc = best_sc
                v = log_fixed_last(current)

        cycle += 1
        if cycle % 2 == 0 or improved_cycle:
            print(
                f"[{label}] cycle={cycle} best={best_sc:.7f} current={cur_sc:.7f} "
                f"beta={beta:.0f} time={time.monotonic() - start:.1f}s",
                flush=True,
            )

    rem = float(time_limit) - (time.monotonic() - start)
    if rem > 15.0:
        v = log_fixed_last(best_a)
        phase = min(30.0, rem * 0.7)
        v2 = lfbgls_minimize_self(v, N, n, SELF_BETAS[-1], idx, phase)
        a2 = softmax_fixed_last(v2)
        sc2 = fft_score(a2, N, n)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
            best_a = a2.copy()
            best_sc = sc2

    print(f"[{label}] final local score={best_sc:.7f} n={n}", flush=True)
    return best_a, best_sc


def exact_score(a):
    """Exact O(n^2) score using np.convolve."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    b = np.convolve(a, a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    mx = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return float("inf")
    val = float(2.0 * float(n) * mx / (s * s))
    return val if np.isfinite(val) else float("inf")


def add_top_candidate(top, a, score):
    """Keep a small list of best candidates by FFT score."""
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return top
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    max_items = 16
    if len(top) < max_items:
        top.append((float(score), a))
        return top

    worst_idx = 0
    worst_score = float(top[0][0])
    for i in range(1, len(top)):
        si = float(top[i][0])
        if si > worst_score:
            worst_score = si
            worst_idx = i

    if score < worst_score:
        top[worst_idx] = (float(score), a)
    return top


def load_initial_sequence():
    """Load the provided parent sequence if available."""
    obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if obj is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(obj, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None

    if arr.size < 32:
        return None

    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None

    if arr.size > MAX_N:
        arr = resample_probability(arr, MAX_N)
    else:
        arr = arr / s
    return normalize_nonneg(arr)


def finalize_sequence(a):
    """Return a valid nonnegative list with sum 1."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        arr = make_uniform(4096)

    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)

    n = arr.size
    if n <= 0:
        arr = make_uniform(1)

    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        arr = make_uniform(n)
        s = float(np.sum(arr))

    arr = arr / s
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    s2 = float(np.sum(arr))
    if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 <= 0.0:
        arr = make_uniform(n)
        s2 = float(np.sum(arr))
    arr = arr / s2

    return [float(x) for x in arr]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Entry point required by the harness.

    Runs a multi-start L-BFGS/smoothed-max optimizer on the parent
    construction and related lengths, then returns the best valid
    nonnegative sequence found, normalized to sum 1.
    """
    global _GLOBAL_DEADLINE

    try:
        total_budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total_budget = 2400.0

    total_budget = max(0.0, min(total_budget, 2400.0) - 45.0)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    _GLOBAL_DEADLINE = t0 + total_budget

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = load_initial_sequence()
    if parent is None:
        parent = make_uniform(4096)

    parent = normalize_nonneg(parent)

    main_len = parent.size
    if main_len > MAX_N:
        main_len = MAX_N
    if main_len < 2048:
        main_len = 4096

    a_main = resample_probability(parent, main_len)
    N_main = next_fast_len(2 * main_len - 1)
    best_sc = fft_score(a_main, N_main, main_len)
    best_a = a_main.copy()
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_a = make_uniform(main_len)
        best_sc = fft_score(best_a, N_main, main_len)

    top = []
    top = add_top_candidate(top, best_a, best_sc)
    print(f"[initial] score={best_sc:.7f} n={main_len}", flush=True)

    tasks = []
    tasks.append((a_main.copy(), main_len, 0.45 * total_budget, "main"))

    l2 = 8192 if main_len != 8192 else 4096
    tasks.append((None, l2, 0.20 * total_budget, "len_alt1"))

    l3 = MAX_N if main_len != MAX_N else 4096
    if abs(l3 - l2) > 200:
        tasks.append((None, l3, 0.15 * total_budget, "len_alt2"))

    l_one = min(main_len, 8192)
    tasks.append((make_one_sided(l_one), l_one, 0.10 * total_budget, "one_sided"))
    tasks.append((make_two_sided_mild(l_one), l_one, 0.10 * total_budget, "two_sided"))

    for start_arr, target_len, frac, label in tasks:
        if time.monotonic() >= _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 30.0:
            break
        remaining = _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 30.0 - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 60.0:
            break

        limit = min(float(frac), remaining * 0.90)
        if limit < 60.0:
            continue

        if start_arr is None:
            a_start = resample_probability(best_a, target_len)
        else:
            a_start = resample_probability(start_arr, target_len)

        a_best, sc_best = local_optimize(a_start, limit, label, rng)

        if np.isfinite(sc_best):
            top = add_top_candidate(top, a_best, sc_best)
            if sc_best < best_sc:
                best_a = a_best.copy()
                best_sc = sc_best
                print(f"[task {label}] new best={best_sc:.7f} n={best_a.size}", flush=True)

    # One extra random-perturbation task if there is time.
    if time.monotonic() < _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 90.0 and best_a.size > 16:
        remaining = _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 90.0 - time.monotonic()
        if remaining > 90.0:
            limit = min(0.10 * total_budget, remaining * 0.85)
            w = log_fixed_last(best_a)
            w = random_smooth_perturb(w, rng, 0.65)
            a_rand = softmax_fixed_last(w)
            a_best, sc_best = local_optimize(a_rand, limit, "random_perturb", rng)
            if np.isfinite(sc_best):
                top = add_top_candidate(top, a_best, sc_best)
                if sc_best < best_sc:
                    best_a = a_best.copy()
                    best_sc = sc_best
                    print(f"[random_perturb] new best={best_sc:.7f} n={best_a.size}", flush=True)

    # Final exact validation for small/medium candidates.
    final_a = best_a.copy()
    top = add_top_candidate(top, best_a, best_sc)

    exact_limit = max(5.0, min(30.0, _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - time.monotonic() - 20.0))
    exact_used = 0.0
    best_exact_a = None
    best_exact_score = float("inf")

    if best_a.size <= MAX_EXACT_N and exact_limit > 5.0:
        est = (float(best_a.size) * float(best_a.size)) / 25000000.0
        if exact_used + est <= exact_limit:
            t_exact = time.monotonic()
            esc = exact_score(best_a)
            exact_used += time.monotonic() - t_exact
            if np.isfinite(esc):
                best_exact_a = best_a.copy()
                best_exact_score = esc

    for sc, a in top:
        if exact_used >= exact_limit:
            break
        if time.monotonic() >= _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 15.0:
            break
        m = a.size
        if m > MAX_EXACT_N:
            continue
        est = (float(m) * float(m)) / 25000000.0
        if exact_used + est > exact_limit:
            continue
        t_exact = time.monotonic()
        esc = exact_score(a)
        exact_used += time.monotonic() - t_exact
        if np.isfinite(esc) and esc < best_exact_score:
            best_exact_a = a.copy()
            best_exact_score = esc

    if best_exact_a is not None and np.isfinite(best_exact_score):
        final_a = best_exact_a.copy()
        best_sc = best_exact_score

    seq = finalize_sequence(final_a)
    print(f"[final] returned n={len(seq)} best_score={best_sc:.7f}", flush=True)
    return seq
```
